<script>
  function selectCustomer(code) {
    const drawer = document.getElementById('customer360Drawer');
    if (drawer) {
      drawer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
    }
  }

  function toggleActionMenu(e, menuId) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="menu-"]').forEach(m => {
      if (m.id !== menuId) m.classList.add('hidden');
    });
    const targetMenu = document.getElementById(menuId);
    if (targetMenu) {
      targetMenu.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="menu-"]').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hidden'));
  });

  const modal = document.getElementById('newCustomerModal');
  const btnOpen = document.getElementById('btnOpenNewCustomer');
  const btnClose = document.getElementById('btnCloseModal');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btnCancelModal');

  if (btnOpen) {
    btnOpen.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.remove('hidden'));
  }
  if (btnClose) {
    btnClose.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.add('hidden'));
  }
  if (btnCancel) {
    btnCancel.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.add('hidden'));
  }
</script></main></div></body></html>

  function toggleImmediatePayment(checkbox) {

    // Tab Switching Logic
    function switchMainTab(tabKey) {
      const treasurySec = document.getElementById('section-treasury');
      const pnlSec = document.getElementById('section-pnl');
      const coaSec = document.getElementById('section-coa');

      const btnTreasury = document.getElementById('tab-btn-treasury');
      const btnPnl = document.getElementById('tab-btn-pnl');
      const btnCoa = document.getElementById('tab-btn-coa');

      // Scroll smoothly to target section
      if (tabKey === 'treasury') {
        treasurySec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      } else if (tabKey === 'pnl') {
        pnlSec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      } else if (tabKey === 'coa') {
        coaSec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      }
    }

    // Chart of Accounts Tree Toggling
    function toggleNode(nodeId) {
      const node = document.getElementById(nodeId);
      const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + nodeId);
      if (node.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        node.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      } else {
        node.classList.add('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(90deg)';
      }
    }

    function expandAllNodes() {
      ['node-assets', 'node-liabilities', 'node-equity', 'node-rev-exp'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + id);
        if (el) el.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      });
    }

    function collapseAllNodes() {
      ['node-assets', 'node-liabilities', 'node-equity', 'node-rev-exp'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(90deg)';
      });
    }

    // Vault quick-select helper
    function prefillTransfer(fromKey, toKey) {
      const fromSel = document.getElementById('transfer-from');
      const toSel = document.getElementById('transfer-to');
      if (fromSel && toSel) {
        fromSel.value = fromKey;
        toSel.value = toKey;
        updateJournalPreview();
        document.getElementById('transfer-form').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }

    // Double Entry Preview updater
    function updateJournalPreview() {
      const fromSel = document.getElementById('transfer-from');
      const toSel = document.getElementById('transfer-to');
      const amountInput = document.getElementById('transfer-amount');

      const debitNameEl = document.getElementById('preview-debit-name');
      const creditNameEl = document.getElementById('preview-credit-name');
      const debitValEl = document.getElementById('preview-debit-val');
      const creditValEl = document.getElementById('preview-credit-val');

      const amountVal = parseFloat(amountInput.value) || 0;
      const formatted = amountVal.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });

      const namesMap = {
        'cash': 'Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø²ÙŠÙ†Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù†Ù‚Ø¯ÙŠØ© Ø§Ù„Ø±Ø¦ÙŠØ³ÙŠØ© (10101)',
        'cairo': 'Ø¨Ù†Ùƒ Ø§Ù„Ù‚Ø§Ù‡Ø±Ø© (10201)',
        'cib': 'Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ù†Ùƒ Ø§Ù„ØªØ¬Ø§Ø±ÙŠ CIB (10202)'
      };

      if (debitNameEl && creditNameEl) {
        debitNameEl.innerText = namesMap[toSel.value] || 'Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø§Ù„ÙˆØ¬Ù‡Ø©';
        creditNameEl.innerText = namesMap[fromSel.value] || 'Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø§Ù„Ù…ØµØ¯Ø±';
      }

      if (debitValEl && creditValEl) {
        debitValEl.innerText = `+${formatted} Ø¬.Ù…`;
        creditValEl.innerText = `-${formatted} Ø¬.Ù…`;
      }
    }

    // Execute transfer & show toast feedback
    function executeTransfer() {
      const toast = document.getElementById('transfer-toast');
      const amountVal = document.getElementById('transfer-amount').value;
      if (toast) {
        toast.classList.remove('hidden');
        toast.classList.add('flex');
        document.getElementById('toast-title').innerText = `ØªÙ… Ù‚ÙŠØ¯ ØªØ­ÙˆÙŠÙ„ Ø¨Ù…Ø¨Ù„Øº ${parseFloat(amountVal).toLocaleString()} Ø¬Ù†ÙŠÙ‡`;
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('hidden');
          toast.classList.remove('flex');
        }, 4000);
      }
    }

    // Print Modal Controls
    function triggerPrintModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('print-modal');
      if (modal) {
        modal.classList.toggle('hidden');
      }
    }
  </script>

<!-- Client-side Interactive Functionality -->
<script>
  (function initReportsHub() {
    const reportCards = document.querySelectorAll('.report-card');
    const currentReportLabel = document.getElementById('currentReportLabel');
    const emptyStateView = document.getElementById('emptyStateView');
    const resultsTableContainer = document.getElementById('resultsTableContainer');
    const generateBtn = document.getElementById('generateReportActionBtn');
    const runIcon = document.getElementById('runReportIcon');
    const resetPreviewBtn = document.getElementById('resetPreviewBtn');
    const refreshSyncBtn = document.getElementById('refreshSyncBtn');
    const syncIcon = document.getElementById('syncIcon');
    const applyFiltersBtn = document.getElementById('applyFiltersBtn');
    const catTabs = document.querySelectorAll('.cat-tab');

    // Tab Switching Micro-interaction
    catTabs.forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        catTabs.forEach(t => {
          t.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-xs');
          t.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        tab.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-xs');
        tab.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
    });

    // Report Selection
    reportCards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        // Remove active ring on others
        reportCards.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('ring-1', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-bright');
        });

        card.classList.add('ring-1', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-bright');
        const title = card.getAttribute('data-report-title');
        if (title && currentReportLabel) {
          currentReportLabel.textContent = title;
        }

        // Smooth scroll to preview panel if clicked from bottom
        currentReportLabel.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
      });
    });

    // Generate Report Trigger with Simulated Loading State
    if (generateBtn) {
      generateBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        generateBtn.disabled = true;
        generateBtn.classList.add('opacity-75');
        if (runIcon) runIcon.classList.add('animate-spin');

        setTimeout(() => {
          if (emptyStateView) emptyStateView.classList.add('hidden');
          if (resultsTableContainer) resultsTableContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
          generateBtn.disabled = false;
          generateBtn.classList.remove('opacity-75');
          if (runIcon) runIcon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
        }, 450);
      });
    }

    // Reset Preview to Empty State
    if (resetPreviewBtn) {
      resetPreviewBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (resultsTableContainer) resultsTableContainer.classList.add('hidden');
        if (emptyStateView) emptyStateView.classList.remove('hidden');
      });
    }

    // Filter Apply Animation
    if (applyFiltersBtn) {
      applyFiltersBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        applyFiltersBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[17px] animate-spin">refresh</span> Ø¬Ø§Ø±ÙŠ Ø§Ù„ØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ«...';
        setTimeout(() => {
          applyFiltersBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[17px]">filter_alt</span> ØªØ·Ø¨ÙŠÙ‚ Ø§Ù„ÙÙ„ØªØ±Ø©';
        }, 500);
      });
    }

    // Sync Button Rotation
    if (refreshSyncBtn && syncIcon) {
      refreshSyncBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        syncIcon.classList.add('rotate-180');
        setTimeout(() => {
          syncIcon.classList.remove('rotate-180');
        }, 600);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></div></body></html>

<script>
  function selectCustomer(code) {
    const drawer = document.getElementById('customer360Drawer');
    if (drawer) {
      drawer.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
    }
  }

  function toggleActionMenu(e, menuId) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="menu-"]').forEach(m => {
      if (m.id !== menuId) m.classList.add('hidden');
    });
    const targetMenu = document.getElementById(menuId);
    if (targetMenu) {
      targetMenu.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="menu-"]').forEach(m => m.classList.add('hidden'));
  });

  const modal = document.getElementById('newCustomerModal');
  const btnOpen = document.getElementById('btnOpenNewCustomer');
  const btnClose = document.getElementById('btnCloseModal');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btnCancelModal');

  if (btnOpen) {
    btnOpen.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.remove('hidden'));
  }
  if (btnClose) {
    btnClose.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.add('hidden'));
  }
  if (btnCancel) {
    btnCancel.addEventListener('click', () => modal.classList.add('hidden'));
  }
</script></main></div></body></html>

  function toggleImmediatePayment(checkbox) {
    const container = document.getElementById('payment-fields-container');
    if (checkbox.checked) {
      container.style.display = 'flex';
    } else {
      container.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  function openPaymentModal(invoiceCode, customerName, remainingAmount) {

  function confirmCancelSuccess() {
    closeModal('modal-cancel');
    showToast('ØªÙ… Ø¥Ù„ØºØ§Ø¡ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ØªÙˆØ±Ø© ÙˆØªØ±Ø­ÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„Ù‚ÙŠØ¯ Ø§Ù„Ø¹ÙƒØ³ÙŠ Ù„Ù„Ø¯ÙØ§ØªØ± Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ø§Ù…Ø©', 'gavel');
  }

  function commitInvoiceSuccess() {
    showToast('ØªÙ… Ø§Ø¹ØªÙ…Ø§Ø¯ ÙˆØªØ±Ø­ÙŠÙ„ Ø§Ù„ÙØ§ØªÙˆØ±Ø© INV-0126 ÙˆØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ« Ø°Ù…Ø© Ø§Ù„Ø¹Ù…ÙŠÙ„', 'verified');
  }

  function showToast(message, iconName = 'check_circle') {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-notification');
    const msg = document.getElementById('toast-message');
    const icon = document.getElementById('toast-icon');

    msg.innerText = message;
    icon.innerText = iconName;

    toast.classList.remove('hidden');
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('hidden');
    }, 4000);
  }

  // Smooth scroll to builder
  document.getElementById('btn-open-creator').addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.getElementById('invoice-builder-card').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  });
</script></main></div></body></html>

    // Tab Switching Logic
    function switchMainTab(tabKey) {
      const treasurySec = document.getElementById('section-treasury');
      const pnlSec = document.getElementById('section-pnl');
      const coaSec = document.getElementById('section-coa');

      const btnTreasury = document.getElementById('tab-btn-treasury');
      const btnPnl = document.getElementById('tab-btn-pnl');
      const btnCoa = document.getElementById('tab-btn-coa');

      // Scroll smoothly to target section
      if (tabKey === 'treasury') {
        treasurySec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      } else if (tabKey === 'pnl') {
        pnlSec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      } else if (tabKey === 'coa') {
        coaSec.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
        btnCoa.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all bg-surface-container-highest text-on-surface shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnTreasury.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
        btnPnl.className = 'px-space-md py-1.5 rounded font-label-md text-label-md transition-all text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface flex items-center gap-1.5';
      }
    }

    // Chart of Accounts Tree Toggling
    function toggleNode(nodeId) {
      const node = document.getElementById(nodeId);
      const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + nodeId);
      if (node.classList.contains('hidden')) {
        node.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      } else {
        node.classList.add('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(90deg)';
      }
    }

    function expandAllNodes() {
      ['node-assets', 'node-liabilities', 'node-equity', 'node-rev-exp'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + id);
        if (el) el.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
      });
    }

    function collapseAllNodes() {
      ['node-assets', 'node-liabilities', 'node-equity', 'node-rev-exp'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        const icon = document.getElementById('icon-' + id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
        if (icon) icon.style.transform = 'rotate(90deg)';
      });
    }

    // Vault quick-select helper
    function prefillTransfer(fromKey, toKey) {
      const fromSel = document.getElementById('transfer-from');
      const toSel = document.getElementById('transfer-to');
      if (fromSel && toSel) {
        fromSel.value = fromKey;
        toSel.value = toKey;
        updateJournalPreview();
        document.getElementById('transfer-form').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }

    // Double Entry Preview updater
    function updateJournalPreview() {
      const fromSel = document.getElementById('transfer-from');
      const toSel = document.getElementById('transfer-to');
      const amountInput = document.getElementById('transfer-amount');

      const debitNameEl = document.getElementById('preview-debit-name');
      const creditNameEl = document.getElementById('preview-credit-name');
      const debitValEl = document.getElementById('preview-debit-val');
      const creditValEl = document.getElementById('preview-credit-val');

      const amountVal = parseFloat(amountInput.value) || 0;
      const formatted = amountVal.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });

      const namesMap = {
        'cash': 'Ø§Ù„Ø®Ø²ÙŠÙ†Ø© Ø§Ù„Ù†Ù‚Ø¯ÙŠØ© Ø§Ù„Ø±Ø¦ÙŠØ³ÙŠØ© (10101)',
        'cairo': 'Ø¨Ù†Ùƒ Ø§Ù„Ù‚Ø§Ù‡Ø±Ø© (10201)',
        'cib': 'Ø§Ù„Ø¨Ù†Ùƒ Ø§Ù„ØªØ¬Ø§Ø±ÙŠ CIB (10202)'
      };

      if (debitNameEl && creditNameEl) {
        debitNameEl.innerText = namesMap[toSel.value] || 'Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø§Ù„ÙˆØ¬Ù‡Ø©';
        creditNameEl.innerText = namesMap[fromSel.value] || 'Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨ Ø§Ù„Ù…ØµØ¯Ø±';
      }

      if (debitValEl && creditValEl) {
        debitValEl.innerText = `+${formatted} Ø¬.Ù…`;
        creditValEl.innerText = `-${formatted} Ø¬.Ù…`;
      }
    }

    // Execute transfer & show toast feedback
    function executeTransfer() {
      const toast = document.getElementById('transfer-toast');
      const amountVal = document.getElementById('transfer-amount').value;
      if (toast) {
        toast.classList.remove('hidden');
        toast.classList.add('flex');
        document.getElementById('toast-title').innerText = `ØªÙ… Ù‚ÙŠØ¯ ØªØ­ÙˆÙŠÙ„ Ø¨Ù…Ø¨Ù„Øº ${parseFloat(amountVal).toLocaleString()} Ø¬Ù†ÙŠÙ‡`;
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('hidden');
          toast.classList.remove('flex');
        }, 4000);
      }
    }

    // Print Modal Controls
    function triggerPrintModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('print-modal');
      if (modal) {
        modal.classList.toggle('hidden');
      }
    }
  </script>

<!-- Client-side Interactive Functionality -->
<script>
  (function initReportsHub() {
    const reportCards = document.querySelectorAll('.report-card');
    const currentReportLabel = document.getElementById('currentReportLabel');
    const emptyStateView = document.getElementById('emptyStateView');
    const resultsTableContainer = document.getElementById('resultsTableContainer');
    const generateBtn = document.getElementById('generateReportActionBtn');
    const runIcon = document.getElementById('runReportIcon');
    const resetPreviewBtn = document.getElementById('resetPreviewBtn');
    const refreshSyncBtn = document.getElementById('refreshSyncBtn');
    const syncIcon = document.getElementById('syncIcon');
    const applyFiltersBtn = document.getElementById('applyFiltersBtn');
    const catTabs = document.querySelectorAll('.cat-tab');

    // Tab Switching Micro-interaction
    catTabs.forEach(tab => {
      tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
        catTabs.forEach(t => {
          t.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-xs');
          t.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        tab.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-xs');
        tab.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
    });

    // Report Selection
    reportCards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        // Remove active ring on others
        reportCards.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('ring-1', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-bright');
        });

        card.classList.add('ring-1', 'ring-primary', 'bg-surface-bright');
        const title = card.getAttribute('data-report-title');
        if (title && currentReportLabel) {
          currentReportLabel.textContent = title;
        }

        // Smooth scroll to preview panel if clicked from bottom
        currentReportLabel.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'nearest' });
      });
    });

    // Generate Report Trigger with Simulated Loading State
    if (generateBtn) {
      generateBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        generateBtn.disabled = true;
        generateBtn.classList.add('opacity-75');
        if (runIcon) runIcon.classList.add('animate-spin');

        setTimeout(() => {
          if (emptyStateView) emptyStateView.classList.add('hidden');
          if (resultsTableContainer) resultsTableContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
          generateBtn.disabled = false;
          generateBtn.classList.remove('opacity-75');
          if (runIcon) runIcon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
        }, 450);
      });
    }

    // Reset Preview to Empty State
    if (resetPreviewBtn) {
      resetPreviewBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (resultsTableContainer) resultsTableContainer.classList.add('hidden');
        if (emptyStateView) emptyStateView.classList.remove('hidden');
      });
    }

    // Filter Apply Animation
    if (applyFiltersBtn) {
      applyFiltersBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        applyFiltersBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[17px] animate-spin">refresh</span> Ø¬Ø§Ø±ÙŠ Ø§Ù„ØªØ­Ø¯ÙŠØ«...';
        setTimeout(() => {
          applyFiltersBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[17px]">filter_alt</span> ØªØ·Ø¨ÙŠÙ‚ Ø§Ù„ÙÙ„ØªØ±Ø©';
        }, 500);
      });
    }

    // Sync Button Rotation
    if (refreshSyncBtn && syncIcon) {
      refreshSyncBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        syncIcon.classList.add('rotate-180');
        setTimeout(() => {
          syncIcon.classList.remove('rotate-180');
        }, 600);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></div></body></html>
